
> rest-express@1.0.0 prebuild
> npm run guard:all


> rest-express@1.0.0 guard:all
> npm run guard:runtime && npm run guard:dead-strata && node scripts/runtime-guards/assert-canonical-topology.cjs && node scripts/runtime-guards/detect-runtime-backups.cjs


> rest-express@1.0.0 guard:runtime
> node scripts/runtime-guards/enforce-canonical-runtime.cjs


=== CANONICAL RUNTIME VIOLATIONS ===
